    1566                 :             : // Per-directory ignore-file lookup for --ignore-files / -g: reads and caches each

    1568                 :             : // later file wins) lazily, and answers the ignore Decision for a path from the ignore

    1571                 :             : //
    1572                 :             : // When gitignore is on and a search root is inside a git repo, the ancestor walk is

    1574                 :             : // the repo root is consulted -- including directories ABOVE the search root -- and the

    2087                 :             : // What a reduction (--summary / --histogram) counts when the walk dives into containers. Diving makes
